Model before rewriting:

find x: int(2..4)
find y: int(2..4)
find z: int(2..4)

such that

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#order_1: bool
  find z#order_2: bool
  find z#order_3: bool
new constraints:
  z#order_1
  or([!(z#order_2),z#order_1;int(1..)])
  or([!(z#order_3),z#order_2;int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3;int(1..)] [2, 4]) >= 2,SATInt(Order, [z#order_1,z#order_2,z#order_3;int(1..)] [2, 4]) <= 4;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
